# misc_tm_43_0001_39_army_data_sheets_for_cartridges_cart  TM 43-0001-39

                   TECHNICAL MANUAL

         ARMY DATA SHEETS
                     FOR

              CARTRIDGES,
 CARTRIDGE ACTUATED DEVICES

                     AND
PROPELLANT ACTUATED DEVICES

                 FSC 1377

                Approved for public release: distribution is unlimited.

HEADQUARTERS, DEPARTMENT OF THE ARMY
                        DECEMBER 1991


---

THE SQUIB SHALL NOT BE EXPOSED TO SOLVENTS OR
ACIDS. THEY SHALL NOT BE SUBJECTED TO SEVERE
SHOCK OR JAR WHICH MIGHT BREAK THE BRIDGE WIRE
RENDERING THE SQUIB INOPERATIVE.

TO PREVENT DISCONNECTED SQUIBS FROM FIRING
BARE ENDS OF LEAD WIRES SHALL BE TWISTED
TOGETHER SO THAT THEY ARE IN FIRM CONTACT WITH
ONE ANOTHER.

THIS ELECTRIC SQUIB SHALL NOT BE RESISTANCE
CHECKED EITHER PRIOR TO OR AFTER INSTALLATION
IN THE DEVICE OF INTENDED APPLICATION.

                     REPORTING OF ERRORS

You can help improve this manual. If you find any mistakes or if you know of a
way to improve the procedures, please let us know, Mail your letter, DA Form
2028 (Recommended Changes to Publications and Blank Forms) to Commander,
US. Army Armament Research, Development and Engineering Center, ATTN:
SMCAR-LSB, Picatinny Arsenal, NJ 07806-5000. A reply will be furnished
directly to you. Comment or questions concerning individual items must be
addressed to the cognizant field activity at Naval Ordnance Station, ATTN:
Code 5320C, Indian Head, MD, 20640-5000. Also see Chapter 1, para 1-1.d.

CHAPTER 3

CUTTERS

3-1. General                                        spring and the release of the firing pin are
                                                    accomplished with one motion when the firing
       A cutter is a cartridge or propellant actu-  pin moves rearward. The sear moves outward
ated device that is used in aerial delivery sys-    to the cutter body section having an enlarged
tems for cargo, helicopter cargo and rescue         internal diameter, which then permits the sear
hoist cable systems and drone recovery sys-         to move sideward to release the firing pin. The
tems. Cutters are classified in accordance with     firing pin is propelled forward under the action
the following characteristics: (1) Method of        of the spring force and strikes the primer,
Actuation - mechanical or electrical, (2)           which ignites the delay element, if applicable,
Function Time - nondelay or delay.                  then the propellant. Under the force of the pro-
                                                    pellant gas pressure, the cutter blade is

       An explosive device used in the AH-1 S     ment.
(MOD) Helicopter Emergency Canopy Removal
System to assure exit from or access to the crew  Functioning:
compartment of the aircraft in an emergency
                                                         Upon actuation of the system by arming

       Major components of the WCA are: mani-     the interconnecting FCDC and SMDC lines to

                                                  force is outward, simultaneously cutting out
                                                  the four plastic window glasses of the crew com-
                                                  partment.

                                                                                                          TM 43-0001-39
CORD, DETONATING, ASSEMBLY, SHIELDED, MILD (SMDC): (MS47)

Type Classification:                              Functioning:

       Refer to aircraft subsystem,                      Upon actuation of the system by arming
                                                  and firing any one of the three armed/firing
Use:                                              mechanisms, a detonation wave is received via
                                                  the junction manifold and is boosted by the
       To provide an interconnecting explosive    loaded booster shell. The stimuli from the
media to the Window Cutting Assembly (WCA)        booster actuates the HNS within the SMDC
of the gunner’s canopy window used in the         assembly,
Emergency Canopy Removal System of the AH-
1 series Helicopter.                                                          NOTE

Description:                                         The MS47 thru MS62 are identical in
                                                     physical appearance and design, The dif-
       Major components of the SMDC Assembly         ference is in length and amount of explo-
are threaded fitting, seals, washers, inner and      sive.
outer ferrules, loaded booster shell, and silver
sheathed mild detonating cord.

(FCDC) and shielded mild detonating cord             tive information pertaining to the inert connec-
